## D-89 — THE SWAP IS LANDED, AND WHAT IT COST WAS THE TEST SURFACE: the marked registers re-grained as written decisions, the boundary row re-homed onto a unit that owns main-side paths, the tab file deleted with its region, and the frozen store left standing

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries, verbatim — *"The swap is solved and blocked only by the test surface that pins the machinery it replaces. This pass re-grains that surface as written decisions, then re-lands the swap"* — driven over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1` from `D-88`'s attempt (`/tmp/d88/attempt/`, taken whole: `view-client.ts` 457, `view-shape.ts` 85, the wiring-and-fixture diff 913). **It supersedes `D-88`'s REFUSAL, never its dated text** (D-46).

**WHAT LANDED.** `src/renderer/view-client.ts` holds the shape and is filled by deliveries; `src/shared/view-shape.ts` carries the two families' vocabulary and the three measured defaults so both realms read one spelling, with `src/main/view-record/index.ts` re-exporting every name (no cite moved). The renderer is wired to `['panes']`/`['focus']`: the drag's two reads are `view.paneSizeOf`/`view.paneBoundsOf` in the caller's own turn, `release` sends ONE `commitPane`, the focus carrier's `persistTarget` sends ONE `commitFocus` and its seam answers `{status:'sent'}`, the boot takes ONE subscription and ONE stated read in `startViewShape`.

**THE RE-GRAIN, PER FILE — THE OLD TERM, THE NEW ONE, AND THE PROOF EACH STILL FAILS.** *`tests/store-focus.test.ts`*: `§2.4`'s observation was "TWO store subscriptions on `mem.focus.entries`/`mem.focus.activeId`" and is now "ONE `onChanged` registration on the crossing, taken in the boot's own function, plus ZERO from the carrier" — the count moves 2 → 1 and the probe reads comment-elided bytes for it; the boot mint's row (`S23-2a`) re-grains from "construction mint-declares the root" to "construction writes NOTHING, and `focus-must-stand` is the declaration that stands in for it" (asserted by name over comment-elided `view-record/index.ts`); `P-SF-SM-1`'s write census moves from "one commit per reference" to "ONE route call carrying the whole mirror, causing ONE push" (the route log is the census); `P-SF-SM-2`'s 16 attempts move onto the one registration and its release; `P-SF-TP-1`/`TP-2`'s hostile arms move from the store's `resolve`/`commit`/`subscribe` to the member's `read`/routes/`onChanged`. *`tests/pane-drag-compliance.test.ts`*: `P-PD-IM-1`'s twelve cells keep their term and change their three STATES — `record` (the family names the pane), `default` (the measured default answers, `source:'default'`), `unfilled` (`filled:false`) — and every write row (`F-5`, `M-5`, `F-6`, `R-4`, `SM-1`, `SM-3`, `M-12`) counts the ROUTE CALL instead of the two-tier commit; `P-PD-TP-2`'s fifth degradation shape becomes `unfilled-shape`. *`tests/store-modules-seams.test.ts`*: `M-PDV-1/2/3/4`, `F-PDV-1(a)/(b)`, `F-PDV-2` and `I-PDV-1` re-point at the shape (and at the backing record for the drag tier's zone pair), and `S-PDV-1`'s static inverts: the composition must carry `view.paneSizeOf(`/`view.paneBoundsOf(` and must NOT carry the retired `layout.pane.${id}.size` — so a revert reddens it. *`tests/fork-store-reads.test.ts`* `S-9`'s admission of the mirror's store-resolve site becomes the stronger claim that the renderer's whole `store.resolve(` surface is EMPTY, with `mirrorStateOf(view)` named as the real subject. *`tests/gesture-session.test.ts`* `F-9(b)` names `src/renderer/view-client.ts` and `src/main/view-record/index.ts` as sibling artifacts (its positive control still fails an unnamed `src/renderer/**` path). **NO REGISTER ROW WAS DELETED TO MAKE A SUITE GREEN.**

**THE BOUNDARY ROW'S RE-HOMING, AND THE ONE THING IT HAD TO KEEP.** `S-1` and the `§4.2 item 8` drive that embedded the same assertion were the two rows of `tests/store-tabs-record.test.ts` that GATE and are never marked (`AGENTS.md`); `strata/next-steps.md` row 1 said their file's retirement required *"re-homing the boundary row onto a unit that owns main-side paths"*, and the retired row DENIED `src/main/` as a prefix — so it could not measure this pass, which edits `src/main/view-record/index.ts`. It now lives at `tests/integration/store-view-record/boundary.test.ts` (`B-1` subset assertion as a SET with no count, `B-2` the row's own falsifiability with three controls, `B-3` the tree reading), over a declared scope of 24 named paths that includes the main-side module **and every file this pass touched**. **DEMONSTRATED RED ON REVERT**: an edit appended to `src/main/mcp-server.ts` — outside the union — reddened `B-1` with `edits outside the union: ["src/main/mcp-server.ts"]` while `B-3` stayed green; the file restored, `B-1` returned green and the battery reads 3/3. A row that cannot fail is a deleted row wearing a new name, which is why `B-2` drives the instrument over synthetic edit sets rather than trusting `B-1`'s today's answer.

**THE RETIREMENT.** `tests/store-tabs-record.test.ts` (100 rows) and `tests/store-tabs-record-register.ts` (its instrument, whose only importer was that file) are DELETED WHOLE — `D-30`'s REPLACED-WHOLE, the crossing's own battery being the successor. `tests/theme-control.test.ts`'s read of the register's `PAGE_NODE_CENSUS_DELTA` was re-pointed at `src/shared/demo-envelope.ts`, the module that AUTHORS the two page ids — a landed source rather than a test register's restatement. **`tests/store-tabs-record-live.mjs` IS UNCHANGED AND RE-POINTED NOWHERE**: its rows reach `getWiredGraphStore`, `closeTab`, `mintTabId` and `driveTabsLandingPage`, and all four still exist and still answer, because the frozen store was not deleted — a row is retired when its subject is gone, not when a later pass is planned. Its header now states, in its own bytes, that **every row is un-run by every leg** (no `scripts` key; the four legs spawn the app instead), which is the fact a green suite cannot show.

**THE FROZEN STORE WAS NOT DELETED, AND STANDING IS MEASURED RATHER THAN DEFERRED.** `src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts` (2,372 lines) is untouched: the swap moves the view record's two families and nothing else, so the store still carries the per-gesture `temp.drag.*` preview, the zone reads behind `candidatesFor` and the `mem.layout`/`file.settings` drag tier, the whole tab machinery, and `driveTabsLandingPage` still runs in `main()`'s boot trace. Deleting it is therefore not green by construction, and the four-clause re-check below is the swap's, not the purge's.

**THE SWAP'S FOUR-CLAUSE RE-CHECK, DRIVEN.** *(1) existence+repair unchanged*: `panes-must-stand`/`focus-must-stand` stand in the boot's ONE `createProfileStore` batch and the crossing's battery is green at 20/20. *(2) the listener fills the shape*: `startViewShape` takes exactly ONE `client.subscribe(...)`, whose body is `fill()` → `read` → `adopt` (one registration, one re-read per push), and `tests/integration/store-view-record/client.test.ts` (10 rows, NEW, gated) drives one push → exactly one read → a WHOLE replacement, and a delivery that costs ZERO writes. *(3) boot data is the file or a repair, the shape is filled from a read, never invented*: `filled: false` before anything answers, the boot's ONE `fill()` is the only read that is not a delivery's, and no member of the client mints, writes or repairs. *(4) every write is an external event or a repair — no timer, poll or write-on-read*: `commitPane`/`commitFocus` are only ever called from `release` and `focusRoute`, the read members touch no route, and over comment-elided bytes `renderer.ts`/`view-client.ts` carry no `setInterval`/`setTimeout`/`requestAnimationFrame`. **A DEFECT FOUND AND FIXED IN THE ATTEMPT**: its `paneGeometryIn` dropped the `status:'committed'` member L2 §2 declares, which reddened the crossing's own gated row — restored, and the crossing battery is green again.

**THE READINGS.** `npm test` **3,043 passed / 2 skipped of 3,045 over 127 files** — 100 rows deleted with the tab file, 13 added (10 `client.test.ts`, 3 `boundary.test.ts`); `typecheck` 0, `typecheck:tests` 0, `build` OK (five artifacts); the four Electron legs on `DISPLAY=:0`: `battery` **184 checks, 0 failures**, `divergence` green (**9 + 4**), `ui` **11/11**, `panes` **7/7**, each with zero survivors after it. `strata/marks.json` REGENERATED: `gating` 165 sites in 24 files (carve-outs `10 → 8`), `on notice` 2,649.

**WHAT IS OWED AND BY WHOM.** `docs/L2/view-record/interface.md` §5's closing two paragraphs still say the swap is *"measured and not taken"*, and `docs/L3/view-record/design.md` describes the renderer's read path as the store's; both are their layers' documents and are **NOT EDITED HERE** (D-3) — the correction is theirs and is named so it is not rediscovered. The frozen store's deletion remains `strata/next-steps.md` row 1's, with the tab machinery's migration as its first step.

## D-90 — THE PURGE'S LAST DELETION IS BLOCKED ON ONE SURFACE: the enumeration is taken whole and four of the five families have a lawful home, but the ZONE family has no successor address, so the store stands and nothing moves

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries — *"move what remains, then delete … This is the last step of `D-78`"* — read against its own stop clause, *"if it is layout data, it needs an address and that is a design act — stop and report rather than inventing one. Same for zones."* **It is a MEASUREMENT AND A REFUSAL, the shape `D-88` took, and it changes nothing in the tree**: `git status --short` is empty at it, and the four legs are re-measured green at the head it leaves (`3,041` passed / `2` skipped of `3,043` over `127` files; `typecheck` `0`; `typecheck:tests` `0`; `build` five artifacts).

**THE ENUMERATION, TAKEN BEFORE ANYTHING WAS TOUCHED — every surface the frozen store still provides, with its site and its callers.** *The tab machinery:* `TABS_ORDER_NAME`/`TABS_LANDING_NAME`/`TABS_LANDING_ID` (`renderer.ts:136/:142/:144`), `TABS_RESERVED_DECLARATION_ROWS` (`:161`), `tabsEntryName` (`:165`), `tabsEntryReadsActive` (`:170`, seven callers), `writeTabsEntryActive` (`:179`, three), `recordEntryKeys` (`:207`), the four per-evaluation referent cells (`:225/:231/:234/:237`), `postNames` (`:241`), `preOrderOfConstraint` (`:250`), `TABS_CONSTRAINT` (`:267-523`, supplied at `:783`), `readTabsRef` (`:552`, four callers), `mintTabId` (`:598`), `closeTab` (`:642`), `driveTabsLandingPage` (`:696`, run in the boot trace at `:1326`). *The store wiring:* `wiredGraphStore` (`:40`), `Tier1StoreSurface` (`:93`), `FILE_TIER_ROOT_NAMES` (`:68`), `bootHandoff` (`:110`), `wiredDeclarations` (`:754`), `buildWiredGraphStore` (`:769`), `getWiredGraphStore` (`:788`), the Y-1 hand-off read (`:1251-1259`), the crossing (`:1272`), `hydrate` (`:1289`), the Y-3 registration (`:1336-1340`). *The drag:* the `tierRead` helper (`:1745-1769`), `renderZone` (`:1799-1805`) and its subscriber (`:1814-1821`), the boot mint (`:1830-1843`), `move`'s `temp.drag.<gestureId>.placement` write (`:1854-1871`), `erasePreview`'s remove (`:1908-1920`), and `candidatesFor`'s two zone reads (`:1937-1939`). *Main side:* `STORE_FILE_GET`/`STORE_FILE_PUT` (`store-channels.ts:19-20`), their handlers (`main.ts:390-394`, `:403-433` with the `file.*` projection loop at `:422-426`), the three preload members (`preload.ts:201-215`) and `STORE_FILE_CHANGED` (`:37`). *The modules and their callers:* `store-core-graph.ts` (2,372), `store-graph-references.ts` (36), the two store-shaped caller turns `overlay-store.ts` (67) / `theme-store.ts` (50); **eight flat suites import the two modules statically** (`store-core-graph`, `store-core-graph-integration`, `store-persist`, `store-security`, `store-focus`, `store-modules-bytes`, `store-modules-seams`, `pane-drag-compliance`), a ninth instrument (`store-core-graph-register.ts`) loads both dynamically, `store-compliance-live.mjs` and `store-tabs-record-live.mjs` reach `getWiredGraphStore` and the tab seams (both un-run by every leg), and `demo/pane-drag-demo/demo.ts:144-166` mints the layout into a store it constructs.

**WHERE EACH ONE GOES, AND THE FOUR-CLAUSE VERDICT.** *(1) THE TAB MACHINERY CONFORMS ON ALL FOUR AND HAS A HOME* — `tabs-client.ts` already holds `read()` and the witness derivation (`deriveTabsWitness`, `:211`): `readTabsRef` becomes the client's reading; `closeTab` becomes the crossing's ONE `remove` route (`preload.ts:172`), whose `order` rewrite and repair are the crossing's own, `order-names-its-tabs` (`tabs-record/index.ts:961-988`) carrying the SAME referent precedence the renderer's repair carries; and `driveTabsLandingPage` becomes the client's witness, whose `resolved` member the boot trace DISCARDS today. **`mintTabId`'s three outside values re-grain rather than transfer.** The crossing refuses ONE seat name, `'order'` (`SEAT_NAMES`, `tabs-record/index.ts:214`, reason `seat-name` at `:201`), so: `reserved-spelling` COLLAPSES INTO `duplicate-id`, because after D-76 the landing entry is an ordinary member and a legal record always names it; `dotted-id` LOSES ITS REASON, because a dot is a character in a member key and no longer a deeper path segment; `malformed-id` is the crossing's own `malformed-row` (`tabs-surface/index.ts:59`, `:67`). **`TABS_CONSTRAINT` dies rather than moves** — the crossing declares `exactly-one-active` (`tabs-record/index.ts:187`, one of the six ids at `:256`). Existence+repair is the crossing's batch, the listener is `onChanged` → re-read (`tabs-client.ts:305-345`), boot data is the record file or a `tabs-must-stand` repair, and every write is the operator's act or a repair. *(2) THE PREVIEW IS NOT DATA* — `temp.drag.*` has ONE writer and no reader outside the rows that assert the write, holds no address, outlives no gesture and crosses no boundary, so it is transient in-realm state: held as plain closure state, with the render turn triggered by the composition's own call. *(3) THE LEGACY PROJECTION DIES WHOLE* — `provident.tabs.read()` and `provident.view.read()` are the boot's data now, and the renderer's `file.*` projection into `['operator-app-state']` is exactly the content D-78 purges. **Nothing on the main side exists only for it except the two handlers, the projection loop and the two preload members**: `settingsSurface.state()`/`.commit()` are ALSO the module registry's path (`main.ts:283`, `:291`, `:304`, `:340`, `:345`, `:347`, `:354`), so both survive beside the app-state address's real writers. *(4) THE ZONE FAMILY HAS NO HOME, AND THAT IS THE BLOCKER* — `candidatesFor` reads `mem.layout.zone.<id>.slot`/`.distance`, the render turn reads `.size`/`.display`; D-87's two roots are the pane geometry (`ViewPaneGeometry`, `view-shape.ts:24`) and the focus mirror, and NO crossing address, declaration or client member carries a zone. Those four values ARE layout data — a zone is not a pane, and the flow's own contract stores zone visibility and size centrally — so they need an address and there is none to move them to. **`createPaneDrag` is not the obstacle**: it takes `frozen` as `unknown` (`:1710`, `:1721`), so it compiles and behaves identically without the store, and it has NO caller under `src/` (one occurrence, its own declaration) because the app's live gesture path is `startGutterAffordance` (`:828-922`), which has no zones at all; the obstacle is that the zone turns would be driven by a test double of the machinery being deleted.

**THE TWO RULINGS THIS PASS CANNOT GIVE ITSELF** (D-78: no layer judges its own design). *(A) DESIGN THE LAYOUT/ZONE FAMILY INTO THE VIEW RECORD* — a third address beside `['panes']`/`['focus']`, a creation-instant declaration, a member on the shape, and the L2/L3 rows that carry it: a unit, and `docs/L2/view-record/interface.md` is at 115/120, so it splits before it grows. *(B) RULE THE DRAG→ZONE FLOW OUT OF THE TREE WITH ITS CARRIER* — `createPaneDrag` keeps the shape's geometry reads, the sink discipline and the two pure caller factories (`zoneSizeConstraint`/`zoneSizeRepair`), and the flow's rows retire whole: the cost is the prebuild baseline's pane-drag composition. **A third path is rejected before it is offered**: keeping a store-shaped duck-typed backing in the composition would leave a third store-like thing standing, against D-78's own number.

**WHAT A LANDING PASS OWES, MEASURED HERE SO IT IS NOT RE-DERIVED.** Deleted whole: `store-core-graph.ts` 2,372 and `store-graph-references.ts` 36, plus — with ruling (A) or (B) — `overlay-store.ts` 67 and `theme-store.ts` 50. Retired whole by D-30's REPLACED-WHOLE, their subject being the module: `store-core-graph.test.ts` 2,496 (69 rows), `store-core-graph-integration.test.ts` 1,501 (44), `store-persist.test.ts` 2,187 (35), `store-security.test.ts` 648 (17), and `store-core-graph-register.ts`. Re-grained, **251 rows over four files** — `store-focus.test.ts` 2,689 (96), `store-modules-bytes.test.ts` 2,058 (48), `store-modules-seams.test.ts` 2,188 (62), `pane-drag-compliance.test.ts` 1,979 (45), the last carrying most of the zone rows. Census and pin sites that count the channels, the bridge or the file: `store-persist`'s export-name census and bridge members, BOTH `isolation` files' `BRIDGE_MEMBERS` (nine, `e2e:51` / `integration:84`), `settings-carrier-retirement`'s `S-11` and `retirement.test.ts:234-249`, `secure-exclusion.test.ts:4802` (`toBe(3)`, its control `4` at `:4841`), `secure-exclusion-register.ts:392` (a `sha256` pin of `store-core-graph.ts`), `tier4-arbitrary-storage-register.ts:76`, `e2e/settings-carrier-retirement/scenarios.test.ts:22` and `bundle-boot-seam`'s `B-5`. `modules.json`'s `graph-store` row (13 endpoints, 9 paths) leaves and `mapRows` reads 21; `brief.md` §2's source count and §6's readings move; the two live drivers retire with their subject; and `tests/integration/store-view-record/boundary.test.ts` — gating, unmarked, never weakened — must gain every path such a pass writes, its declared scope carrying none of the store's files today.

**THE CLOSURE CONDITION IS NOT MET, AND THE MEASUREMENT IS THE SENTENCE.** `grep -rn "createProfileStore(\|storeCreate({\|createGraphStore(" src/` answers three constructions — `main.ts:167`, `operator-settings/index.ts:706`, `renderer.ts:776` — so the tree holds THREE store-like things and not the two D-78 rules, and `strata/next-steps.md` row 1 stays where it is with its work.

**Its register is D-71 to D-89**: the package moved to `1.7.0` with the change push returned to the record root, a repair's credit made explicit, a repair write made to name its commit, the boot battery's teardown made to prove zero, the landing entry's id decided, the landing seat derived and that id moved, the renderer client's Stage A, the two-crossing ruling with its purge, the surplus instance collapsed onto the normal crossing, the `data.*` tools pinned to the central store, the module registry's legacy file dropped rather than migrated with its Q-5 arm, the data-flow convention audited clause by clause, the imperative boot write deleted for a sixth declaration, the `mem` pair's existence made the instance's one declaration, the absence-keyed import moved into the repair, the save put at the landing arm, the purge's two successor addresses declared and batched into the boot's one construction, the renderer swap attempted and refused on measurement, and the swap landed with its test surface re-grained as written decisions. **The package-facing rows among them stay the package's** — `provident-storeroom` is read and never modified, and a defect of it belongs to the unit that surfaced it, filed with symptom, reproduction and proposed fix shape (D-5, D-52).

## D-91 — THE ZONE FAMILY GETS ITS ADDRESS: `['zones']` at `mem`, a third declaration in the boot's one batch, a route and a client read — and the frozen store still stands, because the deletion is the pass after

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's ruling this pass carries, verbatim — *"give zones an address in the view record"*, a third family beside `['panes']` and `['focus']` — which is `D-90`'s ruling (A) and nothing else: that row stopped here because *"it needs an address and that is a design act — stop and report rather than inventing one. Same for zones."* Measured on `20c1ddb` over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1`.

**THE GROUND, MEASURED BEFORE ANYTHING WAS WRITTEN, AND IT DECIDED THE PASS.** **Four members, EACH INDIVIDUALLY OPTIONAL** — `slot` (opaque: the flow's own word for the caller's token) and `distance` (caller-measured) read by `candidatesFor` (`renderer.ts:1937-1939`), `size` and `display` read by the render turn (`:1802-1803`) — and the optionality is driven, not assumed: the demo seeds `size`+`display` for three zones and `slot` alone for a third, while the two zone batteries write `slot`+`distance` and neither of the other two. **ZERO WRITERS UNDER `src/`**: every one of the four reads MISSes at this head, the render turn hands `undefined` straight to its layout call and `candidatesFor` answers `[]`, so the family's only writers in the tree are the demo (`demo/pane-drag-demo/demo.ts:158-166`, `MIN_ZONE_SIZE` 90) and the two test doubles (`tests/pane-drag-compliance.test.ts:490-491`, `tests/store-modules-seams.test.ts:965-966`). **TIER: `mem` ONLY, AND THAT IS WHAT THE ADDRESS TAKES** — `grep -rn "settings\.zone\|file\.[a-z.]*zone" src/` answers **nothing**, so no file-tier copy exists and there is no ascending read to collapse (the demo's one `file.settings.pane.zone-2.size` is a PANE member under a pane id spelled `zone-2`); a `mem` value can never come from a file (`D-84`), so the family's lawful boot source is a **repair re-minted at every boot**, `focus-must-stand`'s precedent. **DEFAULTS: NONE TO INHERIT** — a read that missed answered `undefined`, `candidatesFor` answered `[]`, and the tree states no zone size at all, so a repair minting a geometry would be a second authority. **IDS ARE OPEN** — `zoneIdOf` takes the caller's element's `zoneId` and falls back to `zone-1`, which the render turn hard-codes, so no closed set exists to declare and the placeholder cannot name one. **AND NO CALLER SUPPLIES ZONE INPUT**: `createPaneDrag` takes `frozen` as `unknown` (`:1716`, `:1721`) and has no caller under `src/` at all, so zones reach the composition by store READ alone and there is nothing to reconcile.

**Decision.** `VIEW_ZONES_ADDRESS = ['zones']` at **`mem`**, declared by **`zones-must-stand`** and batched into the boot's ONE `createProfileStore` call beside the tab record's six and this module's two (nine declarations, one construction, unchanged). **The body judges `context.value` and is not wrapped by a null-store guard** — `panesMustStand`'s shape and its measured reason — and its sentence names the fault, *"the zone family does not stand"*. **THE PLACEHOLDER IS THE FAMILY THAT NAMES NO ZONE — `{}` — AND THAT IS THE WHOLE OF THE PAIR'S DESIGN**: it is the one whole legal value that answers every zone exactly as the absent address answered it, so the repair and the read are one authority rather than two; a geometry minted here would be a value no read of this tree ever gave. The repair writes it through `writeWholeValue`, the module's ONE repair-write path, answering the credited arm through `repairAnswer` (`D-72`, `D-73`), and at a landing instant it names its commit at `write`'s fourth position like the other two. `zones-must-stand` is the ninth id of the boot's one namespace and is spelled nowhere else.

**THE FAMILY AS BUILT, SIX FILES AND A CHANNEL SET.** `src/shared/view-shape.ts` gains `ViewZone` (the four members, each optional, `slot` opaque and the other three admitted by their own rule) and `zoneIn(zones, id)` — the PURE derivation, whose missing arm is **`null`** and not a default, because a zone has no measured placeholder to fall back to, and which is re-exported from `src/main/view-record/index.ts` so no cite moves; `src/main/view-record/index.ts` gains the address, the id, the two reason tokens (`malformed-zone`, `malformed-zone-member`), the admission `zoneOf`/`zonesFrom`, the ONE family composer `composeZones` (a supplied member is written over what stands, an absent one keeps it, every sibling zone is kept), the route **`commitZones(id, zone)`** — one zone per act, the family composed AT THE CROSSING so a client one delivery stale cannot erase a zone it never read — the reading's `zones`/`source.zones`, and the third address in the subscription's loop; `src/renderer/view-client.ts` gains the shape's `zones` and `source.zones`, the wire admission, the sent route and **`zoneOf(id)`**, the synchronous read; `src/shared/view-channels.ts` gains `provident:view:zones-commit`; `src/main/view-surface/index.ts` its read answer, its shape arm and its route; and `src/main/preload.ts` with `src/renderer/secure-panels.ts` the fifth route of the `view` member, in lockstep and compared member for member by the battery. **The renderer is NOT wired to it** — `renderer.ts` is byte-unmoved and its four frozen zone reads still address the store — which is this pass's explicit limit and the deletion pass's first act.

**THE ACCEPTANCE BAR, DRIVEN PER CLAUSE IN A NEW GATED BATTERY** — `tests/integration/store-view-record/zone-family.test.ts`, eleven rows, no marker, and named `zone-family` rather than `zones` because `tests/zones.test.ts`'s `R-8` owns that basename and asserts no other `zones*` path exists. *(1) Existence + placeholder at the creation instant*: the store's OWN read answers `{}` when the constructor returns and before any route runs, while a store carrying no batch answers `AddressNotFound` at the same address; the record reads `zones: []` with `source.zones: 'record'`, and `zoneIn` answers `null` for a zone nothing names. *(2) The listener*: one delivery per committed change (1, then 2), **ZERO** for a write at `['elsewhere']`, the SAME subscription delivering again after it, and the withdrawal ending it; and end to end through the surface and the client, one push → one re-read → the shape REPLACED WHOLE. *(3) Boot data*: two successive boots, boot two handed the address ABSENT at the pair's own wrapped context, its repair minting the empty family again, a caller's `zone-1` **not surviving the boot** (measured, `mem`), and the profile byte-identical across both with not one byte of `zones` or of a zone id in it. *(4) Writes*: three caller routes plus the one repair path and no fifth, the handler registered at the boot, and no timer, poll or interval over comment-elided code with a synthetic positive control; and a read proven to land no op. **Three reverts were driven and each answered RED then restored**: the declaration out of the batch → **4 of 11 red** (`Z-1`, `Z-2`, `Z-5`, `Z-10`); the listener's third address removed → **3 of 11** (`Z-3`, `Z-4`, `Z-10`); and `zoneIn` given an invented default → **6 red across two files** (`C-1`, `C-2`, `C-10`, `Z-1`, `Z-4`, `Z-11`), which is the derivation's `null` arm being a measurement rather than a taste.

**WHAT IT COSTS.** `npm test` **`3,041` passed / `2` skipped of `3,043` over `127` files → `3,052` / `2` of `3,054` over `128`** — the eleven rows are this battery's and not one existing row was deleted; `srcTypeScriptFiles` stays `54` (no new source file); `strata/modules.json`'s `store-view-record` row gains the battery's path and its `endpoints` moves **4 → 5** (its cap `90 → 100`), so `mapRows` stays `22`; `strata/marks.json` REGENERATED by `strata-marks.mjs index` — `gating` `165 → 176` sites in `24 → 25` files, the eleven being this battery's, with `on notice` `2,649` and the carve-outs `8` unmoved; and on the dirty tree the same four worktree-boundary rows red by construction — two in `store-core-graph` (`R-9`, `I-11`) and one in each of `gutter-ui` (`I-12`) and `gesture-session` (`F-9(b)`) — green on the committed tree, where the four legs are **0/0/0/0**. **Two layer documents were re-derived rather than appended** (their own layers' documents, corrected here on the user's instruction and not by a layer judging its own design, D-3): `docs/L2/view-record/interface.md` rev 2 **stays at `118/120`** — the trade is stated in its header, the stale §5 ten-line paragraph that said the swap was *"measured and not taken"* being REPLACED by the landed statement plus the zone family's inheritance, which is what paid for the third family's four clauses — and `docs/L3/view-record/design.md` rev 2 reads **`120/130`**, its structure table gaining the two files the swap and this pass added, its stale renderer-read sentence corrected, and its write census moved four → six.

**WHAT REMAINS IS THE DELETION, AND IT IS ONE STEP RATHER THAN A BLOCKED ONE.** `D-90`'s blocker is closed **BY POINTER, never by rewording** (D-46): the enumeration stands as it was measured, and its clause (A) — *design the layout/zone family into the view record* — is what this row lands, so the frozen store's zone reads now have a successor to move onto. Owed to that pass: wiring the render turn's two reads and `candidatesFor`'s two to this family (or ruling `D-90`'s clause (B) out of the tree), then the deletion of `src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts` with the surfaces `D-90` enumerated. **Its register is D-71 to D-90**: the change push returned to the record root, a repair's credit made explicit, a repair write made to name its commit, the boot battery's teardown made to prove zero, the landing entry's id decided, the landing seat derived and that id moved, the renderer client's Stage A, the two-crossing ruling with its purge, the surplus instance collapsed, the `data.*` tools pinned to the central store, the module registry's legacy file dropped rather than migrated, the data-flow convention audited clause by clause, the imperative boot write deleted for a sixth declaration, the `mem` pair's existence declared at the store's creation, the absence-keyed import put inside the repair, `1.9.1`'s landing-arm save, the purge's two successor addresses, the swap refused and then landed, and the enumeration that stopped on the zone family. **The package-facing rows among them stay the package's** — `provident-storeroom` is read and never modified, its own corpus's decision ids are never spelled here, and a defect or a requirement gap of it belongs to the unit that surfaced it, filed with symptom, reproduction, suspected root cause and proposed fix shape (D-5, D-52).

## D-92 — THE FROZEN STORE GOES **UNUSED** AND THE APP IS PROVED UNAFFECTED: the tab machinery onto the client, the zone family onto the shape, `temp.drag` held as closure state, and the marked registers re-grained as written decisions

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries, verbatim — *"this pass makes the store UNUSED and proves parity. The next pass deletes it and rewrites its evidence"* — plus the measurements below, all driven 2026-11-03 on `257e463` over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1`. **IT IS THE FIRST OF TWO STEPS AND ITS SHAPE IS THE TAB RECORD'S OWN SEQUENCE**: crossing, then client, then purge — which is why each of those steps was provable.

**Decision.** **NOTHING UNDER `src/renderer/renderer.ts` READS THE FROZEN GRAPH STORE ANY MORE, AND THE FILE IS NOT DELETED** — `src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts` (2,372 lines) stands, as does every test file, and `strata/next-steps.md` row 1 carries the deletion to the pass after this one. Four wireings landed, each with its old subject named:

- **THE TAB MACHINERY IS THE CROSSING'S CLIENT'S.** `readTabsRef` is DELETED — its three members (`found`/`refused`/`refusedStep`) described the frozen store's own read answer, and `src/renderer/tabs-client.ts` already holds the reading; `mintTabId(order, tabId)` is now PURE over the reading's `order` (no store argument, nothing held, nothing stale); `closeTab(member, tabId)` is ONE `tabs.remove` route call whose `order` rewrite and repair are the crossing's own (`order-names-its-tabs` carries the referent precedence the renderer's repair carried); and `driveTabsLandingPage(reading, runtime)` reads the client's **witness** and **DRIVES EACH AUTHORED PAGE** with one `css.style` state-slice — `display: 'block'` where the terminal's witness stands, `'none'` where it does not. **`TABS_CONSTRAINT` DOES NOT MOVE, IT DIES**: `exactly-one-active` is one of the crossing's six creation-instant declarations, so the frozen store's construction is handed **no `constraints` member at all** — not `[]`, which would still declare that a constraint set exists here (`D-70`'s rule).
- **`mintTabId`'s THREE OLD REASONS ARE RE-GRAINED, AND ONE IS GAINED** — `'malformed-id'` → the crossing's own **`'malformed-row'`** (same subject: a non-string or an empty segment, the store's word now); **`'dotted-id'` IS DELETED AND NOT RENAMED**, because its reason was that a dotted spelling addressed a DEEPER PATH SEGMENT of the frozen store's address space — a tab id is a KEY in `['tabs']`'s one flat family and a dot in a key is a character, so the crossing admits `a.b` and a guard refusing it would refuse a legal member; **`'reserved-spelling'` COLLAPSES INTO `'duplicate-id'`**, which is `D-76` read forward — the landing entry is an ordinary member of `order`, a legal record always names it, so its mint is a duplicate; and **`'seat-name'` IS GAINED** (the crossing's `SEAT_NAME_REASON`): a member and the `order` seat are one name in that family, so an id spelling `order` could not be held.
- **THE ZONE FAMILY IS THE SHAPE'S** (`D-91`'s `['zones']`, wired at last). `candidatesFor`'s two reads and the render turn's two answer `view.zoneOf(id)` synchronously, and the composition's `tierRead` helper — every one of the six reads' single route into the frozen store — is **DELETED**. `renderZone`'s `subscribe('temp.drag', …, {subtree:true})` registration is **DELETED TOO** and replaced by a `renderZone` MEMBER the shape's own delivery calls: the event that changes a zone's `size`/`display` IS the event that renders it, and a second registration would be a second consumer of one push.
- **`temp.drag.*` IS CLOSURE STATE, AND `D-90`'s JUDGEMENT IS LANDED AS ITS OWN TERMS**: one writer, **no reader outside the rows that assert the write**, no address, no persistence, no life beyond its gesture — *"not data, it is closure state: a thing stored because a store had a tier for it, not because anything needed to find it there."* It is one `Map` in the composition's closure, written by `move` and deleted by the erase turn, and the surface's `previewOf` member exists so the at-most-one-erase rule is observable rather than assumed. The store's `commit`-versus-`set` mint vocabulary does not survive, because both arms wrote the same `Map`.

**WHAT THE RENDERER NO LONGER DEPENDS ON, AND WHAT STANDS FOR THE DELETION.** The crossing feed, `hydrate`, the Y-1 hand-off and the Y-3 registration still RUN and feed **nothing** — they mint nodes into a store no read answers and push a signal no consumer wants — and the composition's construction-time `commit`/`clear` bootstrap of `mem.layout`/`temp.drag`/`file.settings` is the ONE write still made to it, kept whole rather than half-removed. **All of them are BOOT STEPS THAT EXIST ONLY TO FEED THE LEGACY STORE, they are NAMED IN THE CODE AS SUCH, and they go with it.** The frozen store's reserved-entry declaration row is the ONE `file.tabs` spelling left in the file, and it is named legacy where it stands.

**THE TIMING, PER WIRED PATH, SINCE EACH ONE MOVED SOMETHING.** The **page drive** is the one real change: it ran synchronously mid-boot off a store nothing read, and it now rides the client's ONE boot read and every `onChanged` delivery, so it lands a tick after `bridge.ready()` — and a record that moves while the app runs moves its terminals. **Nothing between the hand-off and `bridge.ready()` moves** (`bundle-boot-seam`'s `B-5` reads that order). The **close** returns a `sent` statement instead of two store receipts and its answer arrives a tick later; the **mint** is pure and answers in the caller's own turn; the **zone reads and the render turn stay synchronous**, which is what the shape exists for.

**THE MARKED REGISTERS WERE RE-GRAINED AS WRITTEN DECISIONS, AND NOT ONE ROW WAS DELETED** — `tests/pane-drag-compliance.test.ts`'s zone/temp/render rows now drive the shape and the closure (`M-11`, `P-PD-IM-2`, `M-4`, `M-9`, `M-10`, `F-6`, `F-10`, `I-4`, `P-PD-SM-1`, `P-PD-SM-4`, `M-12`), `tests/store-modules-seams.test.ts`'s five (`M-PDV-3`, `M-PDV-4`, `I-PDV-1`, `S-PDV-1`, `REAL-STORE`), `tests/store-focus.test.ts`'s `S22-1` — whose term inverts from "the wiring carries the `file.tabs` landing" to "exactly ONE `file.tabs` spelling survives and it is the legacy reservation row" — and `tests/fork-store-reads.test.ts`'s `S-9`, whose `tierRead` subject is replaced by its successor `zoneOf`. Each kept its bite and gained a control; the two rows that read a store where the shape now answers say so in their own titles.

**THE PARITY BAR — the point of the pass.** The four legs are **0/0/0/0** on the committed tree; `npm test` **`3,052 passed / 2 skipped` of `3,054` over `128` files — BYTE-IDENTICAL TO THE HEAD'S OWN COUNT**, because this pass re-grained registers and added none; and the four Electron legs on `DISPLAY=:0`: `battery` **184 checks, 0 failures**, `divergence` **9 checks, 0 failures** plus **4** extension checks, `ui` **11/11** (`425x22`, `fontSize=16px`), `panes` **7/7** (`22` MCP calls, `2` gestures) — each with **zero survivors** after it, reaped by process tree, with the operator's other Electron applications' processes untouched. **THE TWO PAGES WERE OBSERVED DRIVEN FROM THE RECORD, AND THE READING DISTINGUISHES A GRAPH WRITE FROM A RENDERED ONE**: on a fresh scratch profile the authored `tabs-landing-page` node's `css.style` reads `{display: 'block'}` and the authored `tabs-error-page` node's reads `{display: 'none'}`, from `provident.get_node_state` — the landing terminal stands (one active row and it is `landing`) and the error terminal does not — while **the rendered `style` attribute of both `<section>`s is `''`**, which is what it read before the drive and what the envelope's own `{display:'block'}` produced: **the drive is a measured GRAPH write and NOT a measured DOM difference, and no rendered-surface claim is made.** The `ui` and `panes` legs show the app booting and answering with the drive in place (`11/11` and `7/7`); they carry no page-level assertion, and none is claimed for them.

**IT SUPERSEDES THE FRAMING OF THE ROWS ABOVE IT, NEVER THEIR DATED TEXT** (D-46): `D-90`'s blocker is closed BY POINTER — its enumeration stands as measured and its clauses (1) and (3) are what this row lands, while its clause (2), *"the preview is not data"*, is landed as the judgement above. **Its register is D-71 to D-91**: the change push returned to the record root, a repair's credit made explicit, a repair write made to name its commit, the boot battery's teardown made to prove zero, the landing entry's id decided, the landing seat derived and that id moved, the renderer client's Stage A, the two-crossing ruling with its purge, the surplus instance collapsed, the `data.*` tools pinned to the central store, the module registry's legacy file dropped rather than migrated, the data-flow convention audited clause by clause, the imperative boot write deleted for a sixth declaration, the `mem` pair's existence declared at the store's creation, the absence-keyed import put inside the repair, `1.9.1`'s landing-arm save, the purge's two successor addresses, the renderer swap refused and then landed, the enumeration that stopped on the zone family, and the zone address itself. **The package-facing rows among them stay the package's** — `provident-storeroom` is read and never modified, its own corpus's decision ids are never spelled here, and a defect or a requirement gap of it belongs to the unit that surfaced it, filed with symptom, reproduction, suspected root cause and proposed fix shape (D-5, D-52).

**WHAT THE NEXT PASS OWNS, SO IT IS NOT REDISCOVERED.** The deletion of `src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts` (2,372) and `src/renderer/store-graph-references.ts` (36) with the two store-shaped caller turns and the ~165 rows whose subject dies, the ~800 lines of `renderer.ts` that remain (the hand-off, the hydration, the Y-3 registration, the bootstrap, the reserved row and `wiredDeclarations`), and the 251 registers over the four marked files that still drive a store double where the shape now answers — plus the census re-grains `D-90` sized and `strata/modules.json`'s `graph-store` row. **AND ONE LIVE DRIVER IS OWED WITH IT**: `tests/store-tabs-record-live.mjs` is un-run by every leg, and three of its rows now name the old signatures (`mintTabId(store, …)`, `closeTab(store, …)`, `driveTabsLandingPage(store, …)`); it is stated here rather than edited, because its subject is the store and it goes with it.

## D-93 — THE PURGE'S LAST DELETION IS TAKEN: the frozen graph store is DELETED, its evidence RETIRED OR RE-GRAINED, the closure census reads TWO, and no leg moves

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries, verbatim — *"this pass makes the store UNUSED and proves parity. The next pass deletes it and rewrites its evidence"* — read as the second step, on the head `D-92` left (`505b982`), over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1`. **It executes `D-78`'s purge, `D-90`'s enumeration and `D-92`'s stated debt, and it invents nothing**: every surface it removes has a named successor or a named retirement.

**Decision.** **`src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts` (2,372 lines) and `src/renderer/store-graph-references.ts` (36) are DELETED, and with them every line that existed to construct, hydrate, feed or persist them** — 4,066 lines of `src/` and `tests/` all told, and 165 test rows retired whole. **THE TWO STORE-SHAPED CALLER TURNS GO TOO, ON EVIDENCE**: `src/renderer/overlay-store.ts` (67) and `src/renderer/theme-store.ts` (50) had, measured on this head, **no live consumer under `src/` at all** — the only importers were tests — and each was written against the frozen store's address space (`mem.overlay.<name>.state`, `file.settings.theme.token`), so neither had a subject once that address space went. `D-90` flagged them as a second, smaller ruling and this is it, taken on that measurement rather than on the earlier survey's word.

**WHAT WAS DELETED, WITH ITS LINE COUNT.** `src/renderer/`: `store-core-graph.ts` 2,372 · `store-graph-references.ts` 36 · `overlay-store.ts` 67 · `theme-store.ts` 50 — **2,525 lines**. `src/renderer/renderer.ts` loses `wiredGraphStore`, `buildWiredGraphStore`, `getWiredGraphStore`, `WiredStoreOptions`, `Tier1StoreSurface`, `FILE_TIER_ROOT_NAMES`, `bootHandoff`, `wiredDeclarations`, `TABS_LEGACY_LANDING_NAME`, `TABS_RESERVED_DECLARATION_ROWS`, the `crossing: {put}` seam, the Y-1 hand-off read, the `hydrate(bootHandoff)` call, the Y-3 registration and `PaneDragBacking.frozen` with its `commit`/`clear` bootstrap — its code half is gone and its headstones stand where each stood (headstones are comments and are excluded from every absence census below by the comment-elision rule). `src/main/`: `main.ts` loses the two `ipcMain.handle` sites and the sixteen-line `file.*` projection loop (**what exists on the main side ONLY for the projection is exactly those two handlers, that loop and the preload's members** — `settingsSurface.state()`/`.commit()` SURVIVE because they are also the module registry's path, `D-90`'s clause (3)); `preload.ts` loses the three `store` members and its `provident:store:file:changed` LOCAL; `store-channels.ts` loses `STORE_FILE_GET` and `STORE_FILE_PUT` and keeps `IPC_SECURITY_EXCLUSION`, **its census reading `3 -> 1`**. `src/main/view-record/index.ts`'s collision-rule note is re-grained, `src/shared/types.ts`'s tab-row note likewise. **TWO BRIDGE MEMBERS LEAVE THE LOCKSTEP PAIR** — the `store` namespace is one member of nine, so the set reads **nine -> eight** (`module · notify · onRequest · ready · security · sendReply · tabs · view`), and both `isolation` files' `BRIDGE_MEMBERS`, `settings-carrier-retirement`'s `S-11` and `retirement.test.ts`'s `ISO-7` move with it. **No member replaces it**: the tab record's crossing and the view record's already carry the data.

**WHAT SURVIVED, AND WHY.** The two crossings (`src/main/main.ts`'s `createProfileStore`, `src/main/operator-settings/index.ts`'s `storeCreate`), the three successor families (`['panes']`, `['focus']`, `['zones']`), the tab machinery on `tabs-client.ts`, the pane drag's six shape reads and its `commitPane` route, `temp.drag` as closure state, `zoneSizeConstraint`/`zoneSizeRepair` as pure factories, and the secure crossing's module-registry path. **Two shared mechanism modules now have no importer under `src/`** — `src/shared/overlay.ts` and `src/shared/theme.ts` — and they are NOT deleted here: their subject is the overlay and theme mechanisms, not the store, and their own tests still drive them directly. That is recorded as open, not smuggled into a purge.

## D-94 — THE TWO STALE IMPORTS ARE TAKEN AND THE CROSSINGS GET A LIVE LEG THAT A LEG RUNS: the demo's dead source is DELETED with its checked-in bundle carrying the demo, the headless host's unused import leaves a byte-identical artifact, `overlay`/`theme` are reachable only from tests and STAY OPEN, and `npm run crossings` drives both crossings over a process boundary — `pending.md` row 5 CLOSES

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries, verbatim — *"Job 1 — the import fixes … Job 2 — the live instrument (the owed item) … if no process-boundary route can drive a family — say so plainly rather than reaching into internals"* — read against `D-93`'s three open items *(a)*, *(c)* and *(d)*, which it discharges, and *(b)*, which it measures and leaves exactly where the ruling left it. **It invents nothing**: every path it writes is declared at `tests/integration/store-view-record/boundary.test.ts`, and every count below is a reading of the two runs it took it from.

**THE HEADLESS HOST'S UNUSED IMPORT IS REMOVED AND WHAT IT WAS FOR IS MEASURED, NOT GUESSED.** `src/main/battery-host.ts`'s twelfth line, `import { createProfileStore } from './storeroom.js'`, was introduced with the package's first import (`f349549`) under a comment claiming the seam *"is constructed and held so the import is real in a bundled entry point"* — **and that commit's diff adds the import and the comment and nothing else**: no call site ever existed, so the sentence described an act the file never performed. The removal is ONE LINE and the file's comment block is kept byte-for-byte (it is what points a reader at `src/main/storeroom.ts`, which still carries the seam). **The measurement that makes it free: all five artifacts are BYTE-IDENTICAL across the removal** — `battery-host.mjs` `1,620,613` bytes at `md5 d5bd3f1f…dfe2`, `main.mjs` `1,384,073`, `standalone.mjs` `1,280,979`, `preload.cjs` `6,820`, `renderer.js` `452,535`, every digest unmoved — because esbuild had already dropped an unused import whose module has no top-level side effect (`grep -c storeroom dist/main/battery-host.mjs` → `0` before and after). The `MCP_CLOSURE` censuses of both `isolation` files and of `settings-carrier-retirement` are unmoved too, and that is measured rather than assumed: the closure still reaches `src/main/storeroom.ts` through `src/main/data/journal.ts`'s `DEFAULT_JOURNAL_LENGTH` and `data/store.ts`'s type import, so the sixteen-file closure reads the same sixteen files.

**THE DEMO'S SOURCE IS DELETED, WITH MEASURED EVIDENCE FOR EACH HALF OF THE QUESTION.** *(i) Is `demo.ts` a source of truth for the checked-in `demo.mjs`?* It WAS: the bundle carries esbuild's own source markers (`// src/renderer/store-graph-references.ts`, `// src/renderer/store-core-graph.ts`), the README's Run section gave the exact command (`esbuild … demo-entry.ts --outfile=demo.mjs`), and both files last moved in ONE commit (`ea134a3`). *(ii) Does anything build or run it?* **No**: `grep -rn "pane-drag-demo" package.json scripts/ tests/` answers **zero hits** — no `scripts` key, no leg, no test — and the only mentions in the tree are the map's directory claim and prose. *(iii) Can it compile?* **No**: it imports the store `D-93` deleted (`createGraphStore`, and `GraphStore` as a type), it constructs it, mints the layout into its `mem` tier and reads it back through `store.tiers.mem.get(…)`, and the composition it calls now takes `{view}` where it passes the store. So it is **a stale source with no build path**, and neither leg of the honest fix could be "re-point it": a re-point would have to drive the crossings, which need the app's main process, and this directory's window is a disposable one (`contextIsolation`, no preload) — that demo would be a different demo, and building it is what Job 2 does instead. **So `demo.ts` and `demo-entry.ts` are DELETED — 747 lines and 34, `git` counting 748 for the first because it carries no trailing newline — and `demo.mjs` carries the demo**: it is self-contained (13,217 lines, **zero** `import`/`require`/`export` statements, the two deleted modules inlined), `index.html` loads it as a module, and `run-live.mjs` drives it over CDP, so the directory still runs; its README now opens by stating that its source is gone, that the bundle demonstrates a mechanism this tree no longer has, and that a live instrument for today's panes, tabs and crossings is `npm run panes` / `npm run crossings`. **Neither `.ts` file was compiled by any leg** (`tsconfig.json` includes `src/**` only, `tsconfig.tests.json` `tests/**` only), so this defect was LATENT: the four legs were green over a file that could not compile, which is why `D-93` could state it and not red.

**`src/shared/overlay.ts` AND `src/shared/theme.ts` HAVE NO `src/` IMPORTER AND ARE KEPT — THE FINDING IS THE READING.** `grep -rn "shared/overlay\.js\|shared/theme\.js"` over `src/` answers **nothing**: no file under `src/` imports either. The importers are **tests, and only tests** — `tests/store-modules-seams.test.ts` (`overlayTransition`/`overlayInertDeclaration` and `resolveTheme`/`applyThemeDeclaration`, the only VALUE imports in the tree) plus `tests/overlay.test.ts` and `tests/theme.test.ts`, which take them as **types** and otherwise read their bytes. So those three files are the only thing keeping them alive: `overlay.test.ts` and `theme.test.ts` would still pass without them only if they were rewritten, and `store-modules-seams`' `M-OV-*`/`M-TH-*`/`F-OV-*`/`F-TH-*` rows drive the modules DIRECTLY, which is `D-93`'s own reason for keeping them. **No consumer is planned by anything in the corpus** — the app's theme wiring (`themeWiringRole`) writes its own declaration and imports neither — and **nothing is deleted here**: their subject is the overlay and theme MECHANISMS, not the store, and no ruling covers their disposal (`D-93`'s open item *(b)*). Deleting them on "no importer" would be a purge performed on an absence, which is precisely the move `D-90` refused when it declined to read an unused surface as a dead one. **The finding is the row**: two mechanism modules reachable only from their own tests, awaiting a ruling rather than a census.

**THE OWED INSTRUMENT IS A NEW LEG AND NOT A FOLDING-IN, WHICH COST SIX CENSUSES AND MOVED NO READING.** *(a)* **the choice**: `npm run crossings` → `tests/e2e/store-crossings/crossings-live.mjs`, `package.json`'s fifteenth `scripts` key (the landed set reads `13 → 14`, `ui` still the delta beside it). *(b)* was rejected on evidence, not taste: folding the crossing drives into `npm run panes` would have MOVED that leg's standing reading — `7/7`, `22` MCP calls, `2` gestures, the figures `brief.md` §6 carries and this pass was told to hold — and would have put crossing assertions inside `secure-panels`' own leg, whose seven rows are its unit's (`docs/L1/secure-panels.md` §4.3). **WHAT THE LEG DRIVES, THROUGH THE DOORS THE APP ACTUALLY OPENS — never an internal.** **The normal crossing at its raw address** through the app's own MCP surface: `provident.data.read`/`.write`/`.delete` act on the boot's one `createProfileStore` instance, so a whole-record write at `['tabs']` is that store being written. **The crossing's declared routes** through the preload: `window.provident.tabs.read`/`.commit`/`.remove` run in the RENDERER realm inside a handler body the driver authors and loads with `provident.load` (the `secure-panels` leg's measurement channel; only the EVENT is synthetic). **The change push** through the app's OWN subscriber: the record's delivery drives `driveTabsLandingPage`, and the leg reads the two authored terminals back with `provident.get_node_state` — so the row is "the push crossed the boundary and reached a subscriber", measured at the rendered node. **The secure crossing's three addresses** through the same bridge: `security.set` → `['operator-settings']` (`file`), `module.setDisabled` → `['operator-app-state']` (`file`), `security.setExclusion` → `['operator-exclusion']` (`mem`); the two `file` addresses are then read BY THE DRIVER PROCESS out of `<profile>/provident-operator-settings.json`, and the `mem` one through the crossing and then at a FRESH BOOT. **Three boots, two scratch profiles** — A with no driver envelope (loading one REPLACES the app graph, so the page drive is only observable there), B for the routes, C a second process on B's profile. **IT IS RUN BY A LEG, WHICH IS THE WHOLE POINT**: `D-93` retired two drivers because no leg invoked them and neither could run; this one is invoked by a `scripts` key that builds first, exactly as the four existing Electron legs are.

**ITS MEASURED READING, ON `DISPLAY=:0` AT THIS HEAD, EXIT 0: `18/18` rows, `48` MCP calls — of which `2` are the envelope-settle `provident.list_targets` polls, whose count follows the load's timing, so the DRIVER'S OWN calls read `46`, the figure the leg prints apart from the total — `10` acts dispatched, `9` observation dumps; ## D-95 — THE `view` RECORD'S CROSSING GETS A LIVE LEG A LEG RUNS, THE PANE READ PAIR IS MADE TOTAL WITH A ROW THAT REDS WITHOUT ITS GUARD, AND THE OTHER TWO ITEMS ARE ANSWERED BY MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN BY A DELETION: `overlay`/`theme` do NOT hold the reading the instruction proposed and STAY OPEN, and `demo/pane-drag-demo/` is CORPUS-NAMED and STAYS with its disclaimer

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries, four items, quoted at the point each is answered, read against `D-94`'s four open items on this head (`f918fb4`) over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1`. **It DISCHARGES `D-94`'s *(b)* and *(e)* and SETTLES its *(a)* and *(c)* by finding; it deletes nothing.**

**Decision.** **The view record's crossing gets a live leg INSIDE the leg that already runs** (`npm run crossings`, so no `scripts` key moves and not one of the six key-set censuses does), **the drag's two pane reads are made TOTAL** with the row that owed them going red the moment the guard is removed, **and the two remaining items are answered by measurement**: the first does not hold the reading the instruction proposed, so it is REPORTED rather than purged, and the second is a path the corpus NAMES, so it is KEPT.

- **THE `view` LEG — THE THREE FAMILIES ACROSS A REAL PROCESS BOUNDARY** (`D-94`'s open item *(b)*: *"`store-view-record`'s batteries are its own and no live leg drives that family"*). Ten rows, `V-1`–`V-10`, on a FOURTH and a FIFTH boot of a third scratch profile, through the doors the previous leg established: **`V-1`** the `['panes']` family's EXISTENCE read out of the APP'S OWN PROFILE FILE by the driver process before any act — `<profile>/provident-profile.json` carries `panes` = `{"pane-a":{"size":100,"bounds":{"min":0,"max":200}}}` and spells NEITHER `focus` NOR `zones`, because a `mem` address has no file image; **`V-2`** the crossing's ONE READ answers all three families the creation repairs minted at `source:'record'` — `pane-a` at `100/{0,200}`, the declared empty pair `{entries:[],activeId:null}`, the family naming no zone `[]`; **`V-3`/`V-5`** THE CHANGE PUSH — a subscriber installed on the app's own bridge member (`window.provident.view.onChanged`) FIRED for the write that followed it (`1` delivery read) and its own re-read carried the written size, so what arrived was a delivery and not a timer; **`V-4`/`V-6`/`V-7`** the three ROUTES each driven ONCE and their effect read back — `commitPane('pane-a',{size:240})` committed with the bounds pair KEPT and `240` in the file's own bytes, `commitFocus({entries:[{id:'focus-alpha',target:'/alpha'}],activeId:'focus-alpha'})` committed with that one entry and the active naming it, `commitZones('zone-1',{slot:'slot-alpha',distance:3,size:120,display:'normal'})` committed with all four members back; **`V-8`–`V-10`** THE SECOND PROCESS — `['panes']` SURVIVES (`240`, `source:'record'`, `240` in the file's bytes at that boot) while `['focus']` and `['zones']` DO NOT (the re-minted empty pair and the re-minted empty family, with no `focus`/`zones` key on file), which is `D-84`'s `file`-versus-`mem` distinction measured at the app's own crossings.
- **ITS READING IS PRINTED AND NEVER PINNED: `28/28` rows, exit `0`, `73` MCP calls of which `4` are the envelope-settle `provident.list_targets` polls whose count follows the load's timing — so the driver's own calls read `69` — `17` acts dispatched, `16` observation dumps, over FIVE boots on THREE scratch profiles**, and the run was repeated at this head with the same figures. `brief.md` §6 carries the same decomposition by `D-94`'s rule: a settle-poll total is not stable, so the two figures are printed apart and no row pins one.

- **THE PANE READ PAIR IS TOTAL, AND ITS ROW REDS WITHOUT THE GUARD** (`D-94`'s item *(e)*: *"`startSizeOf`/`boundsOf` are not total over a shape whose `paneSizeOf`/`paneBoundsOf` throw … `readPaneSize`/`readPaneBounds` have no guard; the other six turns do"*). Each now catches its own shape call and answers **what an ABSENT shape answers** — the measured default `100`/`{min:0,max:200}` from the ONE shared declaration — which is the pattern COPIED from those six and not a widening of them (`candidatesFor` answers `[]`, the render turn answers nothing: each its own declared degradation). **PROVED BY A ROW THAT CAN FAIL**: `pane-drag-compliance`'s `F-8/throwing-shape-read` — the row `D-94` recorded the gap in, whose own note said the pair was *"NOT in this row"* — now drives the throwing shape through the drag's START, asserts the default rather than a propagated throw, and keeps the control that a shape which ANSWERS is still believed (`250`/`{10,20}`, figures no default carries). **RED ON REVERT, MEASURED VERBATIM**: with the two guards removed the row answers `AssertionError: the SIZE read absorbs the throw — the seventh turn: expected [Function] to not throw an error but 'Error: boom' was thrown`; restored, the file is `129` passed and the collected count does NOT move, because the pair was brought INSIDE a row instead of given one of its own.

**THE PARITY BAR.** The four legs are **`0/0/0/0`** on the committed tree and `npm test` reads **`2,877` passed / `2` skipped of `2,879` over `124` files — the same figure `D-93` and `D-94` left, because this pass adds no test file and no collected row**; `strata/marks.json` is therefore unmoved (`gating` `175` spec sites in `24` files, `on notice` `2,479`, carve-outs `7`) and `strata-marks.mjs check` is green. The FIVE Electron legs on `DISPLAY=:0` hold every standing figure — `battery` **`184/0`**, `divergence` **`9/0 + 4/0`**, `ui` **`11/11`**, `panes` **`7/7`**, `crossings` **`28/28`** — with **ZERO survivors after every one**, reaped by process tree, and a `ps` census taken before the first leg and after each: **`0`** processes of this repository's throughout, against the SAME four foreign `chrome_crashpad_handler` processes at the SAME PIDs (Discord `4245`, the two snap-chromium `4348`/`4358`, OpenCode `4593`), untouched. **`brief.md` §6's `crossings` row moves in its OWN commit and its line count does not**: the figure is re-written IN PLACE on the line that already carried it, so the file stays at `149` of `150` and NO line is freed — and `pending.md` (`39`), `next-steps.md` (`29`), `strata/records/store-tabs-record-unit.md` (exactly `40`), `docs/L2/view-record/interface.md` (`118`) and `docs/L3/view-record/design.md` (`125`) are untouched, because no declared shape and no tracker row moves.

**WHAT REMAINS OPEN, NAMED RATHER THAN CLOSED.** *(a)* **the `overlay`/`theme` ruling** — the corpus requires both modules and no `src/` importer exists, so their disposal is the user's and not a census's. *(b)* **`security.setExclusion`'s own answer is unread by any instrument** (`D-94`'s item *(d)*, unchanged: the tier the write closes is the tier that would carry the observation). *(c)* **the demo's disposition is now a KEEP on the corpus's naming**, so a future deletion owes the `demo-panes` map row and L1's coverage sentence rather than only a `git rm`. *(d)* **the view record's push has no authored-terminal reading**, so no rendered-surface claim is made for it — the limit is the app's no-op subscriber, not this leg's reach.

## D-96 — THE `overlay`/`theme` RULING IS TAKEN: `theme` IS WIRED AT LAST — its consumer is the app renderer's wiring module, the declaration reaches the AUTHORED node as provident data through ONE `state-slice`, and the driving row REDS on both reverts — while `overlay` is **STOPPED AND NAMED**, because this app authors no overlay to consume it

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the user's ruling, VERBATIM: **"overlay/theme should be consumed by the module renderers (Assuming these are related to the light/dark mode)"** — read against `D-95`'s open item *(a)*, which left exactly this open: *"the corpus requires both modules and no `src/` importer exists, so their disposal is the user's and not a census's."* Measured on this head over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.1`.

**Decision.** **The two mechanisms are NOT legacy and NOT dead — they were OWED a consumer, and one of them now has it.** `theme` is consumed by `src/renderer/renderer.ts`, the app's renderer-wiring module; `overlay` has **no lawful consumer in this tree** and is left standing, with the reason measured rather than assumed (the `D-90` refusal, applied to a module instead of a store).

- **WHAT THEY ARE, ANSWERED FROM THEIR OWN BYTES AND THEIR OWN FILED CONTRACTS — AND THE LIGHT/DARK GUESS IS HALF RIGHT, WITH THE HALF MATTERING.** `src/shared/theme.ts` is a pure total stateless PAIR: `resolveTheme(setting, env)` resolves **a caller-supplied OPAQUE appearance token** against **a caller CLAIM about its own environment** (`env = { prefersDark: boolean }`) into the three-member `ThemeResolution` — `{setting, prefersDark, source: 'env' | 'degraded-env'}` — and `applyThemeDeclaration(attributeName, resolved)` returns `{name, value, removal}`, *"the attribute write it would perform"*, **the name caller-supplied, echoed BY IDENTITY, and the removal case represented as DATA**. **So `theme` IS the appearance resolver behind a light/dark setting — and the module OWNS NO SPECK OF THAT VOCABULARY**: `§1` item 2 is *"the module contains NO `'light'`, no `'dark'`, no `'system'`, no token name, no token value"*, and `prefersDark` is a *"CALLER CLAIM ABOUT AN ENVIRONMENT, never an OS behaviour this repo can observe"* (`§2.3` item 2). The light/dark vocabulary is the CONSUMER's, and the consumer authored here is the demo envelope's own card (`THEME_INITIAL_TOKEN = 'dark'`, the two `theme-set` buttons, the `theme-setting` state node). `src/shared/overlay.ts` is the SIBLING form of the same discipline and is a different mechanism: a **four-state overlay state machine** (`closed`/`open`/`held`/`closing`) over five verb words, `overlayTransition(state, verb, callback?)` → `{state, changed}` — *not* a backdrop and *not* a rendered transition — plus `overlayInertDeclaration(target, attributeName, inert)` → `{name, value, removal, target}`, **the INERT-BACKGROUND declaration** a consumer puts on its background while an overlay is up (`value: 'true'` on the strict-boolean set arm, `false` + `removal: true` on every other input, `target` echoed by identity and read by NOTHING).
- **THE DECLARATION IS DATA FOR SOMEONE ELSE, AND THE CORPUS NAMES WHO.** Both records are `{name, value, removal}` (+ overlay's echoed `target`): the modules take no element, write nothing and own no attribute name, so the applied half is the CONSUMER's by contract (`overlay.md` §2.5 item 2: *"the applied write is the CONSUMER's"*). **And the corpus already names the ROUTE, in the overlay guide's own words**: *"**(a) `props: { inert: 'true' }` on an authored node** — the project-wide UI-constraint route: the attribute rides the authored envelope as pure data"* (`archive/gate-corpus-2026-10-29/guide/overlay.md` § "Making the background inert"). That is the route taken here, and it is the `UI-RENDERED-WITH-PROVIDENT` constraint's own route.
- **THE CONSUMER, IDENTIFIED, AND THE CORPUS NAMED ITS *KIND* BUT NEVER THE FILE.** Two modules in this tree render UI at all: `src/renderer/runtime.ts` (the app graph's producing process, `renderProducingProcess` at `:192`/`:197`) and `src/renderer/secure-panels.ts` (the isolated Security/Debug panes' own Runtime at `:684`). `src/renderer/renderer.ts` is the app renderer's **wiring** module — `demo-envelope.ts:6` calls it *"the renderer module … core-only plumbing"* — and it ALREADY carries both of the appearance control's holders: the authored card lives in the envelope it bootstraps, and `themeWiringRole` (`:1043`) holds the caller-supplied attribute NAME and derives the authored state node's id from the envelope (`§5.1` row 12's ONE bounded wiring role). **`D-94` measured the corpus as it stood — *"No consumer is planned by anything in the corpus"* — and that reading is TRUE OF THAT HEAD AND ITS OWN DATED FINDING, not a prohibition**: what the corpus DOES grant, and what this pass acts on, is the LICENCE, in the mechanism's own contract: *"a later `F1` pass LAWFULLY importing this module must NOT be read as falsifying `U-THEME`. The denial binds this unit's own diff; it does not declare the module permanently importer-less"* (`theme.md` §2.5 item 3). `F1` is `U-THEME-CONTROL`, whose surfaces are in THIS file, so the file is the lawful consumer and no consumer was invented.
- **THE WIRING, AND THE DECLARATION REACHES THE AUTHORED NODE AS PROVIDENT DATA.** `startThemeDeclaration(runtime)` (`renderer.ts:1076`) is held in `main()` at `:821` and driven from the app's OWN change push at `:862` — `themeDeclaration.drive()` sits in `handleRequest`'s notify callback, the one `panels?.refreshDebug(runtime)` already rides (`RH-3` half (b)), BEFORE the notify so a reader of the push sees the graph the declaration left. The drive READS the authored setting (`Runtime.nodeState`, `get_node_state`'s own surface), calls **`resolveTheme`** and **`applyThemeDeclaration`** (the mechanism stays pure: it computes, the wiring applies), and applies the answer as **ONE `state-slice`** — `{kind:'state-slice', node: <derived authored id>, mutation: [{targetProp: 'props.' + declaration.name, mode: 'replace', value: removal ? undefined : declaration.value}]}`. `props.<name>` renders as the bare attribute (`props.resizable` → `resizable`, `demo-envelope.ts`) and an `undefined` value is the engine's declared attribute REMOVAL, so the removal arm is the mechanism's `removal` member and never an empty string that happens to be falsy. **NO hand-written DOM**: no attribute stamp, no class flip, no style write, no created element, no injected CSS — measured green by `gutter-ui`'s `R-13(i)` wiring-token scan, which reds on any of them.
- **IT DECLARES ON A CHANGE AND NOT ON A BOOT, AND THE REASON IS MEASURED.** `props.<name>` reaches **`renderedHtml` AND `ssrHtml`** (measured on a scratch probe: `<div … id="theme-setting" … theme="dark">` in both), and `npm run divergence`'s `SSR fragment matches (structural)` row compares that `ssrHtml` between the REAL app and the headless battery host — which holds no renderer wiring at all (`src/main/battery-host.ts` boots a `Runtime` and its own `invoke` switch). A declaration at boot, or on a reply that moved nothing, would therefore make the two realms render differently and red a leg that exists to prove they do not. So the drive records the authored setting it FOUND (a read, never a write) and declares only when that setting MOVES — which is the ruling's own condition, *a change of state produces the declaration*. **Its one stated limit**: an MCP-originated mutation drives it and a DOM-originated one does not, exactly as the Debug pane's refresh does not; the runtime exposes no post-render hook and this pass claims no new one.
- **PROVED BY A ROW THAT CAN FAIL, AND BOTH REVERTS MEASURED VERBATIM** (`tests/theme-declaration.test.ts`, `6` rows, no `strata:marked` marker so it GATES): `D-96/1` drives the authored `theme-light` handler (`provident.dispatch theme-light click light`), sees the setting move `dark → light`, drives, and finds `props.theme="light"` on the authored node in `renderedHtml` — with the runtime's own `'applied'` beside it; `D-96/2` the removal arm (`''` → `removal: true` → attribute GONE); `D-96/3` a no-op drive moves not one byte of `renderedHtml` or `ssrHtml`; `D-96/4` the three resolved members, and the caller's `env` claim given no precedence over its own token; `D-96/5` the wiring's own presence with two controls; `D-96/6` totality over an unreadable carrier and a throwing write. **REVERT A — the `themeDeclaration.drive()` line removed from the reply notify**: `D-96/5` answers `AssertionError: the declaration is driven by the reply notify: expected false to be true`. **REVERT B — the `state-slice` write removed so the declaration is COMPUTED and never sent**: `D-96/1`, `/2`, `/3`, `/5`, `/6` red, the first being `AssertionError: the runtime's OWN answer to the state-slice write: expected 'no-answer' to be 'applied'`. Restored, the file reads `6 passed`.
- **THE 157 ROWS, DISPOSITIONED ROW BY ROW: FOUR RE-GRAINED, AND NOT ONE CONTRADICTED.** **Unchanged: all `69` of `tests/overlay.test.ts`** (whole subject `overlay.ts`, which this pass does not touch — its `X-5` "no overlay surface under `src/**`" and `X-6` "ZERO importers" are both still TRUE) **and all `58` of `tests/theme.test.ts` but ONE**: `R-6`'s no-importer half asserted *"zero importers, not one"* (`D-93`'s rollback form) and is now FALSE, so it is **re-grained** to a CLOSED SET WITH ONE NAMED MEMBER — `src/renderer/renderer.ts`, exact-membership, with its bounded-extension control kept intact and the `D-93` form kept visible beside. The same form is taken by **`tests/store-modules-seams.test.ts`'s `S-TH-1`**, **its `E-TH-1`**, and **its register row `P-SMS-TH-IM-1`'s `tree-census` attempt**, each re-grained to one admitted carrier by name with the planted-consumer control unchanged; and by **`tests/theme-control.test.ts`'s `P-TC-TP-3` attempt 12**, whose allowed set gains the one path and whose bounded-extension control still proves a SECOND carrier is a FINDING — plus one new assertion that the admitted member is MEASURED rather than merely allowed. **The register's count does not move** (still `62` collected rows in `store-modules-seams`, `58` in `theme`, `61` in `theme-control`, `69` in `overlay`): every re-grain is an in-place term, and `theme-control`'s `P-TC-TP-3` attempt 11 — *"the wiring role holds NO mechanism reference"* — stays TRUE and untouched, because the new role is a SEPARATE declaration and `themeWiringRole` remains the deliberately inert name-holder `§2.4` item 2 requires.
