## D-107 — THE REFUSAL LEDGER'S `log` IS FORWARDED, AND THE LAST DEVIATION CELL IS RE-READ AS `N/A`: the package's refusal arm has always handed `{status, message, log, address}`, so the ledger carries that list on the passing arm's own member name and through the SAME one accessor — ENUMERABLE there where the passing arm's is not, present where a store answered and absent where this module composed the sentence — while `data.*`'s clause-2 cell is measured against the three facts it rests on and recorded as no deviation at all; the two prose corrections, `ST-F1`'s re-read at the installed `1.9.2`, and the residual list as it now stands

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** six residuals taken in the order this pass's instruction names, measured on this head over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.2`, one fresh instance per arm.

**THE FOUR FACTS ABOUT THE REFUSAL'S MEMBER, EACH MEASURED, AND THE THIRD IS THE ONE THAT DECIDES THE CODE.** *(1) THE PACKAGE HAS ALWAYS HANDED IT*: a `data.*` write refused by a family's value arm throws a value whose own names are exactly `['status','message','log','address']`, and the log names the pair — `constraint-check: panes-must-stand` · `outcome` — so the list `D-103` recorded as discarded is on every refusal MEASURED — `ConstraintPassRefused` from a write and from a delete, `ParentAnchorMonotonicityViolation`, `AddressMalformed` — and not on some. *(2) IT IS ENUMERABLE, WHICH IS THE ONE THING THAT DIFFERS FROM THE PASSING ARM*: `Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(caught,'log')` reads `{enumerable:true, writable:true, configurable:true}` against the passing record's `enumerable:false`, so `Object.keys`, `{...caught}` and `JSON.stringify(caught)` all CARRY it — and the surface still copies it, because the answer must carry nothing that aliases the store's release (G10) and because a refusal that reached no package arm at all carries no list. *(3) `[]` IS AN ANSWER HERE AND IT MEANS "NO PASS RAN"*: a refusal composed before any pass — `ParentAnchorMonotonicityViolation`, `AddressMalformed` — carries `log: []`, which is exactly the `D-103` warning read the other way (there `[]` was not the clean arm's answer; here it is the pre-pass arm's), so the two arms are told apart by the presence of a `storeStatus` and never by a length. *(4) ONE ACCESSOR SERVES BOTH, AND NO SECOND WAS MINTED*: `commitLog` is a PROPERTY READ with an `Array.isArray` guard and a per-entry copy, and a property read finds a non-enumerable own member exactly as it finds an enumerable one — so `refusalLedger` calls it and nothing in `src/main/data/` learns the member's name (`D-103`'s `L-6` still reads `0` for `.log` in all seven of the module's files).

**Decision.** **THE MEMBER IS `log`, ON THE LEDGER, OPTIONAL, AND FORWARDED WHERE THE PACKAGE HANDED ONE.** `DataLedger` gains `log?: readonly DataLogEntry[]` — the SAME name, the SAME plain enumerable array and the SAME `DataLogEntry` shape the two mutating verbs already answer with, so a caller reads ONE vocabulary off both arms and no word is minted. **IT IS OPTIONAL AND THE OPTIONALITY IS THE MEASUREMENT AND NOT A HEDGE**: the six refusals this module composes itself — `malformed-arguments`, `declared-tier`, `volatile-path`, `root-not-writable`, `cursor-unknown`, `store-unreachable` — are answered before any store is consulted, so an `[]` there would be this surface's claim about a pass that never began; `refusalLedger` therefore spreads the member only where `commitLog` answered an array, the same rule `storeStatus` follows one member below it. `read`, `list` and `journal` are untouched — a read consults no constraint, which is `D-103`'s reading and stands.

**THE ROWS AND THE REVERT, DRIVEN.** `tests/integration/central-data/message-log.test.ts` gains **two, unmarked and GATING** (`D-40`, and the file `D-103` made is where the same member's second arm belongs): `L-7` drives a `data.write` at `['panes']` carrying `{junk:true}`, asserts the refusal's `log` is an ARRAY, an ENUMERABLE own member of the ledger, TWO entries in order (`constraint-check` naming `panes-must-stand`, then the refusal's own `outcome` at `state:'KC-3'`), each entry the package's five keys and no sixth, the log crossing `JSON.stringify` intact — the form the MCP caller receives, since `mcp-server.ts` answers `JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)` — and, as its control the other way, the SAME store's passing write still ending at `commit` while the refused one reaches none; `L-8` drives the five module-composed arms and asserts each carries NO `log`, with the store-answered refusal on the same surface carrying one as the control. **THE REVERT: DROP THE MEMBER FROM `refusalLedger` AND 2 of 8 RED** (`L-7` whole, and `L-8`'s control), restored and green.

**THE `data.*` CLAUSE-2 CELL: THREE FACTS MEASURED, AND IT IS `N/A` AND NOT A DEVIATION.** **ONE ACT, NOT TWO.** The survey that preceded `D-106` concluded the cell is `N/A`; that row recorded it as the one cell standing; the two readings were never reconciled, and they are reconciled here by the pointer clause at `D-106`'s own row rather than by rewording it (`D-46`). ***(i) NO SUBSCRIBER***: `listen(` is called at THREE sites in `src/` and by the three modules that declare the addresses — `src/main/tabs-record/index.ts:1264` binds `[TABS_RECORD_ADDRESS]`, `src/main/view-record/index.ts:1368` binds `['panes']`, `['focus']`, `['zones']`, `['theme']`, `src/main/operator-settings/index.ts:1041` binds `['operator-settings']`, `['operator-exclusion']`, `['operator-app-state']` — eight addresses over three subscriptions, with `register(` the same count beside each; while `src/main/data/` calls `listen` **0** times and `register` **0** times, so a `data.*` write at a caller-chosen path fires no delivery and one at a declared root fires THAT FAMILY's listener, which re-reads the family it already owns. ***(ii) NO RENDERER ANSWER SITE***: `data.` occurs **0** times in `src/renderer/renderer.ts` and **0** times in `src/main/preload.ts`, and its ONE occurrence in `src/main/main.ts` is a comment (`:27`). ***(iii) NO MCP RESOURCE AND NO NOTIFICATION***: `ALL_RESOURCES` is THREE and names `data` nowhere, and the tree's only `sendResourceUpdated` targets `mcp://provident/app` with its only caller `src/main/main.ts:561` on the graph push. **THE READING, AND IT IS THE SURVEY'S: `data.*` is a tool surface over caller-chosen paths and carries no family of its own, so there is nothing for it to carry** — at every address it may lawfully write that a crossing declares the family's own listener already delivers, and at every other address the caller that just wrote is the only consumer there is, answered synchronously by the receipt it holds. **THE DEVIATIONS COLUMN IS THEREFORE EMPTY AT ALL SIX OF `D-82`'S FAMILIES ON ALL FOUR CLAUSES**, and `strata/pending.md` row 1 — which IS the revival condition — now says so: the cell becomes a deviation again the day a consumer exists, and the fix then is a **NEW ADDRESS IN A FAMILY'S LISTENER SET**, never a listener hung off `data.*`.

**`ST-F1` RE-READ AT THE INSTALLED `1.9.2`: IT STANDS, AND IT IS THE KERNEL'S OWN TWO READERS THAT DISAGREE.** The surviving clause was filed at `1.6.1`; measured one fresh instance per arm on `1.9.2`, a value whose `message` and `repair` are INHERITED (`Object.create({message, repair})`, `Object.hasOwn` false for both) **REGISTERS** — the registration path reads `'repair' in outcome`, a `[[HasProperty]]` that walks the prototype chain (`kernel-registration.mjs:399-401`), invokes the inherited repair ONCE and lets the batch stand — while the SAME value at the WRITE instant is read by `Object.hasOwn(outcome,'message')` and `ownMember(outcome,'repair')`, a `[[GetOwnProperty]]` that finds neither (`kernel-attempt.mjs:810-818`), so the repair does not run and the write answers `ConstraintPassRefused` with *"returned a value outside the three declared outcome arms"*; the controls are an OWN `{message, repair}` that repairs and lands at the write instant, and the inherited arm that registers. **So the version line moves and no claim does**: the finding is re-filed against `1.9.2`, upstream-owned and not patched here (`D-5`, `D-52`), with the suspected root cause — `1.9.0`'s narrowing of the write-time arm to own members was never applied to the registration-time arm — and the proposed fix shape — that arm's predicate changed to `Object.hasOwn` — recorded in `strata/records/store-tabs-record-unit.md` §1, which stays at exactly `40` because the `1.6.1` "not documented" bullet is REFLOWED from two lines to one and no clause, quotation or dated reading is removed.

## D-108 — `ST-F1` CANNOT BITE THIS APP, AND THAT IS NOW A DRIVE RATHER THAN A READING: all THIRTEEN declarations the two crossings register build their refusals as OBJECT LITERALS whose `message` and `repair` are OWN at both instants, `VA-14` drives every one of them on the very objects the store adopted, `VA-15` reddens on a planted `Object.create({message, repair})` pair — and the population the corpus has never carried is recorded: ELEVEN `src/shared/` modules, `3,579` lines, unreachable from all five entries, every one of them DECLARED and not one of them rot

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-03 · **Source:** the instruction that `ST-F1` be made structural here — "the strongest form is a drive" — and that the modules nothing imports be censused and classified; measured on this head over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.2`, one fresh instance per arm.

**THE GROUND, QUOTED, AND IT IS ONE SHAPE THIRTEEN TIMES.** A declaration's `constraint` is ONE function object: the batch is the array `src/main/main.ts` hands `createProfileStore` and the write path invokes the SAME stored function, so "own at both instants" is "is the value this function RETURNS built with own members". **THE TAB RECORD'S SIX**: `tabs-must-stand` returns `{message: 'the tab record does not stand', repair: mintRecord(getStore)}` (`src/main/tabs-record/index.ts:960-963`); `order-names-its-tabs` binds `const refuse: TabsConstraintAnswer = {message: ORDER_SEAT_MESSAGE, repair: repairOrderSeat(getStore)}` once per invocation and returns it (`:1009`); the three field pairs return `{message, repair: healRecord(store, liveHints(store))}` (`:875`, `:884`, `:894`); and `exactly-one-active` returns `{message: <one of its three sentences>, repair: healRecord(store, {removals, written, removed, preOrder})}` (`:813-821`). **THE VIEW RECORD'S FOUR**: the existence arms `{message: 'the pane geometry does not stand', repair: repairPanes(getStore)}` (`src/main/view-record/index.ts:940-943`), `'the focus mirror does not stand'` (`:979-982`), `'the zone family does not stand'` (`:1020-1023`) and `'the appearance record does not stand'` (`:1066`) beside its staleness arm `{message: THEME_STALE_MESSAGE, repair: repairTheme(getStore)}` (`:1069`) — while the `D-105` value arms return `{message: PANE_UNUSABLE_MESSAGE}` and its two siblings (`:955`, `:991`, `:1032`), a BARE arm that must carry no `repair` at all. **THE SECURE CROSSING'S THREE**: `{message: EXCLUSION_ABSENT_MESSAGE, repair: mintExclusion}` (`src/main/operator-settings/index.ts:805`), `{message: RECORD_ABSENT_MESSAGE, repair: (c) => mintRecord(c, userDataDir)}` (`:823`) and `{message: APP_STATE_ABSENT_MESSAGE, repair: (c) => mintAppState(c, userDataDir)}` (`:842`), each beside a bare value arm (`:809`, `:827`, `:846`). **THIRTEEN OBJECT LITERALS, NOT ONE SPREAD OF A SHARED BASE AND NOT ONE BASE OBJECT CARRYING THE MEMBERS** — and the only `Object.create` in the three modules is `Object.create(null)`, the caller-data copy map, which has no chain to inherit through (`operator-settings/index.ts:173-176`).

**THE ROW MAKES IT STRUCTURAL, AND IT IS A DRIVE** (`tests/integration/central-data/value-arms.test.ts`, two rows, unmarked and GATING — `D-40`). `VA-14` constructs the central store with the app's REAL batch and KEEPS the arrays, so the declarations it drives are the objects the store registered and not look-alikes rebuilt beside it; it asserts SIX + FOUR + THREE = THIRTEEN, the ten ids as one namespace, and then EXERCISES EACH PAIR'S REFUSAL ARM and asserts the ANSWER: `Object.getPrototypeOf(answer) === Object.prototype` FIRST and separately (a base would pass an own-`message`-only probe and still fail the write instant — the divergence is exactly that gap), `Object.getOwnPropertyNames` exactly the arm's declared members, `Object.hasOwn` for each, `typeof repair === 'function'` where the arm is repairable, and `'repair' in answer` FALSE on the six bare arms, because an INHERITED `repair` is what a `'repair' in outcome` probe finds and what a `{message}` arm must not have. **FOUR OF THE THIRTEEN ARE DRIVEN THROUGH THE BATCH'S OWN HOLDER AND THE ROW SAYS SO**: `exactly-one-active` and the three field pairs read the STANDING RECORD, and a violation they judge can only stand inside a commit — every write that would leave one is repaired in that same commit, and the package consumes the answer and never hands it back — so the getter the SAME declaration objects close over is pointed at the record such a commit would see. Nothing is rebuilt and nothing is faked except the handle's answer, which is the one thing no caller can observe otherwise. **AND THE WRITE INSTANT IS MEASURED WHERE THE KERNEL IS THE ONLY READER**: an own `repair` is INVOKED inside the caller's own commit, so the six tab pairs' repair is made to run through a corrupt whole-record write (`repaired:true`), and each value arm's refusal is read for ITS OWN family sentence with `ST-F1`'s divergence sentence asserted absent from all of them.

**IT CAN FAIL, AND THE PLANT IS IN THE SOURCE RATHER THAN IN A FIXTURE.** `VA-15(a)` registers a planted declaration whose refusal is `Object.create({message, repair})` and measures `ST-F1` at this head rather than quoting it: the **registration instant ACCEPTS it** — the kernel reads `'repair' in outcome`, invokes the INHERITED repair once and lets the batch stand — while the **write instant refuses it `ConstraintPassRefused` with *"returned a value outside the three declared outcome arms"* and NO `repair-run` entry in the log**. ## D-109 — THE BUMP TO `provident-storeroom@1.9.3`, `ST-F1` DISCHARGED UPSTREAM, AND ITS INSTRUMENT INVERTED RATHER THAN DELETED: the kernel's two instants now ask ONE own-member question, so the divergence `D-107` re-read and `D-108` made structural has no second instant left — `VA-15` becomes the registration refusal it now is, with its two reverse probes and a class instance, `VA-14`'s stale clauses are corrected around an untouched drive, and every figure a bump can move was re-measured and none did

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-04 · **Source:** the user's instruction this pass carries — the published `1.9.3` taken, every live pin moved in place, `VA-15` inverted rather than deleted, `ST-F1` discharged in its own record, the two tracker sentences it would falsify corrected, and the census re-measured rather than carried — measured on this head over the installed `provident-storeroom@1.9.3`, one fresh instance per arm.

**THE BUMP, AND THE LOCK IS WHAT PINNED IT.** `package.json` read `"provident-storeroom": "^1.9.2"` — a CARET RANGE that already admitted `1.9.3`, so the range decided nothing and the lock was the whole of the pin; the install was `npm install provident-storeroom@1.9.3` with a scratch cache, never a name resolved off the range. **ELEVEN CELLS MOVED TOGETHER AND ALL SIX LOCK NODES CARRY `1.9.3`**: the SIX `version` cells (the main package and its five platform packages) and the FIVE exact `optionalDependencies` entries, with the SIX `resolved` tarball URLs and the root `dependencies` spec moving beside them — read as `1.9.3` at `package.json:28` and `package-lock.json` lines `14`, `25`/`26`, `41`/`42`, `57`/`58`, `73`/`74`, `89`/`90`, `2353`/`2354` and `2361`–`2365`, with NOT ONE `1.9.2` left in the file. **THE PINS MOVED IN PLACE, THE PIN FOLLOWING THE REVIEWED INSTALL AND NEVER THE OTHER WAY** — the corpus's own habit: `tests/storeroom-surface.test.ts`'s literal and the comment above it, re-derived to `1.9.3`'s own subject (the two instants agreeing) with its closing claim KEPT TRUE; `strata/brief.md` §2's pinned row and its reason sentence; and `src/main/storeroom.ts`'s census header, where the VERSION moved and no figure did.

**THE CENSUS IS UNMOVED, RE-MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** One fresh instance over the installed bytes reads `Object.getOwnPropertyNames(store)` → **14** own string members in the order the header pins, `getOwnPropertySymbols` → **13** at construction with `Symbol(provident-storeroom.registration-save)` fifth, **14** after one `write` (`Symbol(provident-storeroom.kernel)`), the closed status set **20**, and the tiers `mem` · `file` · `temp` — every figure `1.9.2` read, and `RELEASE-NOTES.md` §1.9.3's "what does not move" paragraph names the same set.

**`ST-F1` IS DISCHARGED, AND THE ONE-WORD-HALF CORRECTION IS THE PART THAT MATTERS: UPSTREAM NARROWED THREE READS WHERE THE FINDING PROPOSED ONE.** `RELEASE-NOTES.md` §1.9.3 item 2, quoted: *"a constraint whose `message` or `repair` the returned shape carries on a prototype no longer registers a repair the write then refuses to read, so the failure surfaces where the constraint is authored rather than at the first write."* **THE CODE IS TWO MEMBERS PLUS A RETURN**: `kernel-registration.mjs:399-400` reads `Object.hasOwn(outcome,'message')` and only then `ownMember(outcome,'repair')` — the finding proposed the `repair` probe alone, and the `'message' in outcome` guard beside it would still have admitted an own `repair` paired with an inherited `message` — and `:676` reads the repair's RETURN's `ok` by `ownMember` too. `kernel-attempt.mjs` is byte-identical and `index.d.ts`'s whole diff is one docblock, which is why no other module in this tree moves.

**`VA-15` IS INVERTED, NOT DELETED, AND IT KEEPS EVERYTHING THAT MADE IT REAL** (`tests/integration/central-data/value-arms.test.ts`; unmarked and GATING, `D-40`). **(a)** the SAME plant — the same `Object.create({message, repair})` value, the same real declaration answering it, the same scratch profile — now measures the registration instant's FIXED behaviour: the construction THROWS `ConstraintRefusedAtRegistration`, the refusal's own names are exactly `["status","message","log","address"]`, its `log` is `[]`, and the base's `repair` — counted, which `1.9.2` could NOT assert because it was invoked once — is invoked **ZERO** times. **THE TWO REVERSE PROBES ARE ADDED**, because they are what distinguishes a whole fix from the one-word half: own `message` + inherited `repair`, and own `repair` + inherited `message`, each refused at registration with the inherited member reached zero times (and the second's OWN repair zero too, the kernel refusing before it runs a member that shape really carries). **AND THE CLASS INSTANCE** — `get message()` on the prototype with an own `repair`, `1.9.2`'s quietest acceptance — is refused with them; a fifth arm pins the third narrowed read, an own pair whose RETURN carries an inherited `ok`, refused with a log of `constraint-check` · `repair-run` and its repair run once. **THE DIVERGENCE'S SECOND INSTANT IS NOW UNREACHABLE AND IS NOT ASSERTED** — no store exists, so there is nothing to write to and a `null` store would prove nothing. **(b)** the static half over comment-elided code is **byte-for-byte** as filed.

**ITS BITE WAS PROVED AND THEN REVERTED, BECAUSE THE PACKAGE MAY NOT BE TOUCHED.** With `1.9.3`'s two narrowed members unchangeable from here, the row's subject was removed instead: the plant's DRIVEN refusal was made own at BOTH members — the minimum that removes it, since narrowing either member alone is still refused, measured — and the construction then REGISTERED with the repair invoked once, reddening the refusal clause (*"expected false to be true"*) and, with the count hoisted above it, the zero-invocation clause (*"expected 1 to be +0"*). Both temporary edits were reverted and the row re-greened: `15 passed` of `15`.

**`VA-14`'S DRIVE IS UNTOUCHED; FOUR PROSE CLAUSES ARE STALE AND ONE OF THEM READ BACKWARDS.** (i) the header paragraph that described the registration path as reading `'repair' in outcome`; (ii) the assertion message that would have printed a FALSE diagnostic — *"an `Object.create({message, repair})` answer REGISTERS and then answers every write with…"* — a sentence no longer true of any instant; (iii) the clause calling registration *"the instant ST-F1 says is the permissive reader"*, which reads backwards: from `1.9.3` it is the STRICTER arm, since it demands an own `message` before it reads a repair at all and refuses the CONSTRUCTION where no own repair answers; (iv) the absence assertion's rationale, which credited a `'repair' in answer` probe the chain could win, and — corrected with it because it was the same defect one paragraph down — the block preamble that said an inherited `repair` on a `{message}` arm *"is invoked at the registration instant and IGNORED at the write instant"*, true at `1.9.2` and false at `1.9.3`, where NO instant reads one. Not one assertion, context or drive changed.

**THE TWO TRACKER SENTENCES THAT WOULD HAVE BECOME FALSE, CORRECTED IN PLACE AND WITHOUT A LINE.** `strata/pending.md:23` — *"What it did not discharge — the two instants still disagree on an inherited `repair` — stays live"* — now records the `1.9.3` discharge with its measurement, the file holding at **40**; `strata/next-steps.md:27` — *"`ST-F1` stays live in `strata/records/store-tabs-record-unit.md` §1"* — now says it was DISCHARGED and that the record carries it, the file holding at **29**.

**`ST-F1` IS DISCHARGED IN ITS OWN RECORD, AND THE CEILING IS HONOURED BY FREEING BEFORE SPENDING.** `strata/records/store-tabs-record-unit.md` gains ONE bullet in `CD-F3`'s own shape — *"⟶ DISCHARGED 2026-11-04 by the installed `1.9.3`, `RELEASE-NOTES.md` §1.9.3 item 2: …"* — carrying the both-members-and-the-return correction, the four measured arms and the unreachability of the second instant; the line it spends was FREED FIRST by merging §1's free-standing continuation into the paragraph it continued (`D-108`'s own clause), so not one clause is dropped and only a line break goes: **`40 → 40`**. ## D-111 — THE RULING ON THE ELEVEN UNIMPORTED `src/shared/` MODULES IS THE USER'S AND IT IS **CARRIED AS DESIGNS**: the population `D-108` measured stands as declared surfaces awaiting a caller — ratified rather than left as a measurement nothing gates — with what carrying leaves unsaid stated in the same row, the three follow-ups it was conditioned on discharged, and `strata/pending.md` row six gone because its work landed

**Status:** ACTIVE · **Date:** 2026-11-04 · **Source:** the user's ruling, given against the two dispositions `D-110` put in that row's own `Waits on` cell — *"carried as designs"* and *"retired with their contracts and batteries"* — with the three follow-ups named in the same breath as the condition of the first.

**THE RULING, QUOTED, AND WHOSE IT IS.** The word taken is **"carried as designs"**, against the alternative **"retired with their contracts and batteries"**, and **it is the USER'S and not a layer's**: `D-108` measured the population, `D-110` made the row ready to take one word, and neither took it, because deleting a declared deliverable whose contract asserts it is unimported would falsify that contract and redden its own battery, while giving one a consumer is what `D-90` refused to invent. A ruling is not a measurement and this row does not dress it as one.

**WHAT CARRYING ASSERTS, AND IT IS THREE THINGS.** *(i)* **AN UNIMPORTED MODULE MAY BE A DELIVERABLE** — nothing in this corpus makes reachability from an entry point a condition of a thing being a thing. *(ii)* **A CONTRACT CAN BE COMPLETE, TESTED AND CORRECT WHILE NO ENTRY POINT REACHES IT** — which is what seven of the eleven asserting their own unimportedness in their own bytes means: that is the state those contracts were authored to describe, not one they tolerate. *(iii)* **THE ELEVEN STAND AS SURFACES A CALLER — this app's, or a fork's — MAY ONE DAY ADOPT**: `census` · `container` · `layout-projection` · `menu-template` · `mount-invariant-guard` · `overlay` · `owned-list-host` · `path-fork-cycle` · `relocate` · `slot-host` · `zones`, `3,568` lines by a `wc -l` over the eleven files (`D-108`'s own instrument read `3,579` over the same population — ONE population and two instruments, and neither figure is rewritten). **THE MEASUREMENT THIS RESTS ON IS `D-108`'s OWN SENTENCE**: *"AN UNIMPORTED DELIVERABLE IS A DESIGN, AND NOT ONE OF THESE IS AN ORPHAN"* — reached by resolving the four import forms over comment-elided bytes from all five entry points and finding eleven unreachable, not one of them unclaimed as a row of `strata/modules.json`.

**WHAT CARRYING LEAVES UNSAID, STATED RATHER THAN HIDDEN.** Nothing **EXPIRES**: no clause here sets a date by which a consumer must arrive, and a ratified design has no expiry to miss. Nothing **RE-MEASURES**: the reading above is this pass's, and no instrument re-takes it, so it is a dated reading and never a standing one. And **NO INSTRUMENT WOULD NOTICE one of them drifting from the platform it was written against** — the eighteen files that pin them run under a layout-less DOM shim and every one reports ON NOTICE, so a `provident-storeroom` bump or an envelope change could leave a contract asserting a shape the platform no longer has while every leg stays green. **They are RATIFIED, NOT WATCHED**, and a reader who takes this row as a watch has read it wrong.

**THE TWO CAVEATS, NAMED — NEITHER A DEFECT, BOTH FACTS A CORPUS THAT SAYS "DESIGNS AWAITING A CONSUMER" SHOULD ALSO SAY.** *(i)* `src/shared/owned-list-host.ts:295-296` and `src/shared/slot-host.ts:313` COMPOSE `mem.list.*` and `mem.slots.*` — the address space of the frozen graph store `D-93` DELETED — so their one specified integration target no longer exists, and what remains is the lawful caller-supplied handle both contracts already declare. *(ii)* `src/shared/path-fork-cycle.ts` IS DUPLICATED BY ITS OWN CONSUMER: `tests/e2e-battery.test.mjs:503` defines its own `pathForkCycle` with different CSS — `background-color: hsl(…)` there against the module's per-level `10px`/`20px` — so **the fixture the gating leg runs is not the module**, and the module's own `pathForkCycleLegacyData` has no caller at all.

**THE POPULATION, AS `D-108` MEASURED IT, AND THIS ROW ADDS NO FIGURE TO IT.** ELEVEN modules; SEVEN contracts asserting the unimportedness in their own bytes (quoted at that row); TWO more asserting it in a note beside the contract rather than in it — `listhost.md:1144`'s diff-scope paragraph and `slothost.md:166`'s evidence-status paragraph, the pair `D-108` counted as silent and this row does not; and TWO asserting nothing about importers at all (`mount-invariant-guard`, `path-fork-cycle`). EIGHTEEN batteries pin them and **EVERY ONE carries `strata:marked-file`**, so the whole population reports **ON NOTICE** and nothing gating pins it — bar **ONE gating carve-out**, `tests/menu-template.test.ts:992`'s `R-13`, a no-importer probe that ASSERTS the unimportedness and would have to assert its opposite the day a consumer lands.

**THE THREE FOLLOW-UPS, EACH DISCHARGED, AND EACH IS A CLAUSE ABOVE RATHER THAN A PROMISE.** *(a)* **THE DISPOSITION IS RECORDED** — this row: the ruling quoted and ascribed, what carrying asserts, and what it leaves unsaid. *(b)* **THE ONE FALSE CLAIM IN LIVE CODE IS CORRECTED, AND IT WAS IN `src/`, NOT IN THEM**: `src/renderer/renderer.ts:1793` read *"… the candidate/slot read feeding the PURE `withinProximity` comparator …"* and now says the composition IMPLEMENTS THE SEAM SHAPE `src/shared/relocate.ts` DECLARES WITHOUT IMPORTING IT (`CandidateFor`), which is what `PaneDragSurface`'s own docblock said all along; the comparator has no call site under `src/` and its only importer in the tree is a battery's own drive (`tests/pane-drag-compliance.test.ts:136`). **A comment can move a built artifact and this one did** — `renderer.js` `475,374` → `475,848` bytes, md5 `8d559143…` → `b2c5a850…`, the other four byte-identical — so the five Electron legs were re-run rather than inherited. *(c)* **THE TWO CAVEATS STAND IN THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE**, one clause each.

**WHAT IT COSTS, MEASURED ON THE COMMITTED TREE, THE BOUNDARY ROW DECLARING EVERY PATH AND GAINING NO ENTRY BECAUSE EVERY ONE WAS ALREADY DECLARED ABOVE.** `npm test` `2,938` passed / `2` skipped of `2,940` over `131` files — not one row added and not one file, so `strata/marks.json` is NOT regenerated and `strata-marks.mjs check` is green UNREGENERATED at `236` gating sites in `31` files, `2,479` on notice, the `7` carve-outs unmoved; `typecheck` `0`, `typecheck:tests` `0`, `build` OK at `preload.cjs` `8,354`, `main.mjs` `1,408,722`, `standalone.mjs` `1,282,773`, `battery-host.mjs` `1,622,407` and `renderer.js` `475,848`; `strata-walkthrough.mjs refs` resolves every `D-n` at `111` rows; `strata-lint` reads `47` PASS / `1` FAIL before and after, the register's seven over-length rows being the one FAIL this corpus ACCEPTS at `D-110` and does not chase. **THE FIVE ELECTRON LEGS WERE OWED AND RUN, and no count is restated here** — `brief.md` §6 carries them, and it carries no artifact byte figure for this row to have moved, so nothing there is rewritten.

**IT SUPERSEDES BY POINTER AND NEVER REWORDS** (`D-46`). `D-108`'s split, its `3,579` and its *"eleven DECLARED deliverables and zero rot"* stand as the readings of their own date and are read THROUGH this row; `D-110`'s *"the eleven unimported modules on the ruling row six now waits for"* is a dated statement of what waited and the wait is over; `D-90`'s refusal to invent a consumer is untouched, since carrying invents none. **ITS REGISTER IS `D-71` TO `D-110`** — from the change push's return to the record root, down through the repair writes, the boot battery's teardown, the landing entry's id, the renderer's tab client, the two-crossing ruling, the purge of the frozen graph store and its re-homed boundary row, the crossings and view legs, the deliveries and the refusal ledger, the unreachable population itself, the `1.9.3` bump with `ST-F1` discharged, to the pass that made row six ready to take one word — as prose and never as a bare `D-n`.
